# Findings: which routes can align the rare-earth-free hard phases, and how far

Generated by `./regenerate.sh` from commit `1f634cc6f724` (inputs committed). Every number in this
file is produced by that command from the sourced records in `data/`; nothing is typed by
hand. Method: `docs/METHOD.md`. Evidence levels are stated per record (an abstract is an
abstract; a datasheet range is a range).

## 1. What the work establishes

- **The account**: 31 phases (all 24 of the 24 rare-earth-free candidate phases named in 114 review records, plus the Nd2Fe14B reference) × 18 route classes (all 16 classes used or proposed in the literature, plus 2) = 558 cells: 103 reach A ≥ 0.95, 30 are capped by geometry, 196 cannot align, 229 are decided by a named unpublished quantity.
- **Held-out test** (v1, frozen at 15c80aa): the predicted ceilings held for 428 of 441 held-out measurements (13 certain violations, 34 within the Js conversion range); 'cannot align' was refuted in 12 of 130; 10 of 10 field/no-field pairs moved as predicted. Every failure is diagnosed in §6.
- **Revised rules on later held-out batches** (frozen before each opening, sources never used before): v2: 4 of 4 ceilings held; v3: 89 of 89 ceilings held; v4: 38 of 40 ceilings held; v5: 60 of 60 ceilings held (failures diagnosed in §6).
- **Settled by the held-back data**: in Ti-free AlNiCo 5 the α1 rods are not held on the parent <100> nearest the field: 2 held-out equiaxed magnets exceed the 0.831 that this would allow (up to 0.895 with the same-sample Js), and in columnar crystals A falls with the cosine of the field angle; the account now lets the rods follow the field (ceiling 1 for any grain). Where the three rod directions are equivalent (no bias, or a field along <111> or <110> that ties them), spinodal alloys (AlNiCo, Fe-Cr-Co, the alnico-like HEA) keep more remanence than independent rods allow: that reading was refuted 26 times, while the bound for one coupled network per grain (0.866 for random grains, with the no-field easy directions <111>) held every time.
- **The rod rule differs by alloy, as the account says**: equiaxed field-annealed AlNiCo 8/9 (rods on the <100> nearest the field, cap 0.832) stays at or below 0.790 in all 51 held-out cases with a same-sample Js or Mr/Ms; equiaxed Mo-free Fe-Cr-Co (rods follow the field in any grain, ceiling 1) exceeds that cap in 12 held-out cases (up to 0.980).
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.61** (2 cells: tau-MnAl FS, tau-MnAl-C FS) — non-cubic parent crystals align in the melt (sign of their susceptibility anisotropy decides the axis), then transform with equal variant weights.
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.61** (2 cells: tau-MnAl DS, tau-MnAl AM) — parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]: the product variants' easy axes lie at 52.1 deg to the fibre axis; massive (incoherent) growth: product keeps at most the OR of its nucleation grain -> A between 0.50 (random) and 0.614 (OR kept).
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.61** (2 cells: tau-MnAl TGG, tau-MnAl-C TGG) — templates grow in the parent, giving it a fibre texture.
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.61** (2 cells: tau-MnAl-C DS, tau-MnAl-C AM) — parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]: the product variants' easy axes lie at 52.2 deg to the fibre axis; massive (incoherent) growth: product keeps at most the OR of its nucleation grain -> A between 0.50 (random) and 0.613 (OR kept).
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.83** (16 cells: L10-FeNi FA, L10-FeNi SA, L10-FePt FA, L10-FePt SA, L10-CoPt FA, L10-CoPt SA, L10-FePd FA, L10-FePd SA …) — untextured parent: each grain keeps the variant nearest the bias axis.
- **Geometric cap A_max ≈ 0.83** (1 cells: AlNiCo-8-9 AM+FA) — AM parent without texture (published); Tc 1163 K above the whole formation window 1083-1113 K.

## 2. The magnet each phase could become

Br_max = Js · A_max at full density (Js at 300 K). "Established" routes are those whose
ceiling does not depend on an unpublished quantity; "open" routes are listed with the
ceiling they would reach if the deciding quantity falls their way (see §5 and REPORT.md).
Today's practice for comparison: sintered Nd–Fe–B (field-pressed), Br = 1.23–1.53 T in the known cases, A = 0.73–1.00 (Js(Nd2Fe14B) -/+5 %).

## 3. The account, phase by phase

Routes (18 classes, every one evaluated for every phase):

| route | definition |
|---|---|
| FP | field alignment of free particles, then consolidation |
| MO | mechanical (shape-induced) orientation of anisometric particles, no field |
| FS | solidification in a magnetic field |
| DS | directional solidification / columnar casting / melt spinning, no bias |
| AM | additive manufacturing, no bias |
| FA | formation in a magnetic field from an untextured parent |
| DS+FA | columnar parent + formation in a field |
| AM+FA | printed parent + formation in a field |
| RX+FA | rolled/recrystallised parent + formation in a field |
| SC+FA | single-crystal parent + formation in a field |
| SA | formation under stress from an untextured parent |
| DS+SA | columnar parent + formation under stress |
| RX+SA | rolled/recrystallised parent + formation under stress |
| SC+SA | single-crystal parent + formation under stress |
| HD | hot deformation of the hard phase |
| HDDR | reversible disproportionation-recombination with texture memory |
| EP | epitaxial or template-defined growth (films, wire arrays; not bulk) |
| TGG | templated grain growth during sintering |

### τ-MnAl (binary, L1_0)

Js (300 K) = 0.820 T; K1 = 1.000 MJ/m³; Tc = 642 K.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| FP | full | 1.000 | 0.820 | single-variant particles each turn their easy axis to the field |
| MO | open | 1.000 | 0.820 | shape of single-crystal particles and its relation to the easy axis unpublished |
| FS | capped | 0.614 | 0.504 | non-cubic parent crystals align in the melt (sign of their susceptibility anisotropy decides the axis), then transform with equal variant weights |
| DS | capped | 0.614 | 0.504 | parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]: the product variants' easy axes lie at 52.1 deg to the fibre axis; massive (incoherent) growth: product keeps at most the OR of its nucleation grain -> A between 0.50 (random) and 0.614 (OR kept); no bias, equal variants |
| AM | capped | 0.614 | 0.504 | parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]: the product variants' easy axes lie at 52.1 deg to the fibre axis; massive (incoherent) growth: product keeps at most the OR of its nucleation grain -> A between 0.50 (random) and 0.614 (OR kept); no bias, equal variants |
| FA | open | 0.899 | 0.737 | untextured parent: each grain keeps the variant nearest the bias axis; Tc 642 K below the formation window 723-973 K; paramagnetic bias <= 16 kT at 2 T, up to 3.9e+02 kT at 10 T only if dchi ~ chi (upper bound) |
| DS+FA | open | 0.972 | 0.797 | parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]; Tc 642 K below the formation window 723-973 K; paramagnetic bias <= 16 kT at 2 T, up to 3.9e+02 kT at 10 T only if dchi ~ chi (upper bound) |
| AM+FA | open | 0.972 | 0.797 | parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]; Tc 642 K below the formation window 723-973 K; paramagnetic bias <= 16 kT at 2 T, up to 3.9e+02 kT at 10 T only if dchi ~ chi (upper bound) |
| RX+FA | open | 0.972 | 0.797 | parent texture unpublished; Tc 642 K below the formation window 723-973 K; paramagnetic bias <= 16 kT at 2 T, up to 3.9e+02 kT at 10 T only if dchi ~ chi (upper bound) |
| SC+FA | open | 1.000 | 0.820 | single-crystal parent; Tc 642 K below the formation window 723-973 K; paramagnetic bias <= 16 kT at 2 T, up to 3.9e+02 kT at 10 T only if dchi ~ chi (upper bound) |
| SA | capped | 0.899 | 0.737 | untextured parent: each grain keeps the variant nearest the bias axis; coherent shear/ordering strains at both steps (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| DS+SA | full | 0.972 | 0.797 | parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]; coherent shear/ordering strains at both steps (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| RX+SA | open | 0.972 | 0.797 | parent texture unpublished; coherent shear/ordering strains at both steps (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| SC+SA | full | 1.000 | 0.820 | single-crystal parent; coherent shear/ordering strains at both steps (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| HD | none | 0.500 | 0.410 | carbon-free MnAl gains no anisotropy by warm working; τ decomposes |
| HDDR | none | 0.500 | 0.410 | no constituent forms a hydride stable at HDDR temperatures (>= 1023 K) at bar pressures |
| EP | full | 1.000 | 0.820 | geometric ceiling of a single-variant epitaxial film (A = 1); no epitaxial film of this phase is reported, so the route is unproven (not a bulk magnet) |
| TGG | capped | 0.614 | 0.504 | templates grow in the parent, giving it a fibre texture; without a bias the product then forms as from a DS parent: parent fibre [0, 0, 0, 1]: the product variants' easy axes lie at 52.1 deg to the fibre axis; massive (incoherent) growth: product keeps at most the OR of its nucleation grain -> A between 0.50 (random) and 0.614 (OR kept); no bias, equal variants |

| DS+SA | open | 1.000 | 0.516 | uniaxial (shape) anisotropy of the annealed HEA (elongated ferromagnetic precipitates) unpublished; if it is c: orientation of the elongated precipitates (along the field, or on the nearest <100>) unpublished. If precipitates follow the field: nucleation without an orientation relationship: no coherent strain to bias. If precipitates on the nearest <100>: parent texture unpublished; coherent transformation with a tetragonal strain (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| RX+SA | none | 0.500 | 0.258 | nucleation without an orientation relationship: no coherent strain to bias (same under either orientation relationship hypothesis); without alignment the coupled α1 network (no-field easy directions <111>) keeps up to A = 0.866 (by analogy with alnico; bound held in every held-out unbiased case) |
| SC+SA | open | 0.999 | 0.516 | uniaxial (shape) anisotropy of the annealed HEA (elongated ferromagnetic precipitates) unpublished; if it is c: orientation of the elongated precipitates (along the field, or on the nearest <100>) unpublished. If precipitates follow the field: nucleation without an orientation relationship: no coherent strain to bias. If precipitates on the nearest <100>: single-crystal parent; coherent transformation with a tetragonal strain (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| HD | none | 0.500 | 0.258 | the hardening precipitates form only in the final anneal (as in alnico) (same under either orientation relationship hypothesis); without alignment the coupled α1 network (no-field easy directions <111>) keeps up to A = 0.866 (by analogy with alnico; bound held in every held-out unbiased case) |
| HDDR | none | 0.500 | 0.258 | the anisotropic microstructure forms only in the final anneal; a hydrogen cycle before it acts on the parent only (same under either orientation relationship hypothesis); without alignment the coupled α1 network (no-field easy directions <111>) keeps up to A = 0.866 (by analogy with alnico; bound held in every held-out unbiased case) |
| EP | open | 1.000 | 0.516 | uniaxial (shape) anisotropy of the annealed HEA (elongated ferromagnetic precipitates) unpublished; if it is c: geometric ceiling of a single-variant epitaxial film (A = 1); no epitaxial film of this phase is reported, so the route is unproven (not a bulk magnet) (same under either orientation relationship hypothesis) |
| TGG | open | 0.577 | 0.298 | uniaxial (shape) anisotropy of the annealed HEA (elongated ferromagnetic precipitates) unpublished; if it is c: orientation of the elongated precipitates (along the field, or on the nearest <100>) unpublished. If precipitates follow the field: templates grow in the parent, giving it a fibre texture; without a bias the product then forms as from a DS parent: product nucleates without an orientation relationship: parent texture not inherited. If precipitates on the nearest <100>: templates grow in the parent, giving it a fibre texture; without a bias the product then forms as from a DS parent: DS growth texture of the parent unpublished |

### L1_0 (FeCoNiMnCu)Pt high-entropy intermetallic

Js (300 K) = 0.302 T; K1 = 0.200 MJ/m³.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| FP | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | depends on whether single-variant particles can be made |
| MO | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | shape of single-crystal particles and its relation to the easy axis unpublished |
| FS | none | 0.500 | 0.151 | parent crystals are cubic: isotropic susceptibility, no torque in a field |
| DS | open | 0.577 | 0.174 | DS growth texture of the parent unpublished |
| AM | open | 0.577 | 0.174 | AM growth texture of the parent unpublished |
| FA | open | 0.832 | 0.251 | untextured parent: each grain keeps the variant nearest the bias axis; Curie point of the forming phase unpublished (formation window 873-873 K) |
| DS+FA | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | parent texture unpublished; Curie point of the forming phase unpublished (formation window 873-873 K) |
| AM+FA | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | parent texture unpublished; Curie point of the forming phase unpublished (formation window 873-873 K) |
| RX+FA | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | parent texture unpublished; Curie point of the forming phase unpublished (formation window 873-873 K) |
| SC+FA | open | 0.999 | 0.302 | single-crystal parent; Curie point of the forming phase unpublished (formation window 873-873 K) |
| SA | capped | 0.832 | 0.251 | untextured parent: each grain keeps the variant nearest the bias axis; coherent transformation with a tetragonal strain (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| DS+SA | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | parent texture unpublished; coherent transformation with a tetragonal strain (stress sign chosen to suit) |
| RX+SA | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | parent texture unpublished; coherent transformation with a tetragonal strain (stress sign chosen to suit) |

| HD | open | 1.000 | 0.302 | deformation texture of the phase unpublished |
| HDDR | none | 0.500 | 0.151 | no constituent forms a hydride stable at HDDR temperatures (>= 1023 K) at bar pressures |

| tau-MnAl | FP | full 1.000 | Mr/Ms of field-aligned, single-variant τ-MnAl (binary, L1_0) particles fixed in resin, measured along the alignment axis at 300 K after saturation (demagnetisation-corrected): predicted to reach >= 0.95; refuted if, with particles shown single-variant by EBSD, it stays below that. |
| tau-MnAl | MO | open 1.000 | Measure particle habit (shape vs easy axis) by SEM + EBSD of single-crystal particles; it selects the ceiling (plates, easy axis normal: A_max = 1.000, rods, easy axis along rod: A_max = 1.000, equiaxed: A_max = 0.500). |

| tau-MnAl | DS | capped 0.614 | Easy-axis pole figure (EBSD or XRD) of τ-MnAl (binary, L1_0) formed from a DS parent of measured fibre texture, converted to A along the best axis: refuted if A > 0.64 (the cap is geometric: no bias acts). |
| tau-MnAl | AM | capped 0.614 | Easy-axis pole figure (EBSD or XRD) of τ-MnAl (binary, L1_0) formed from a AM parent of measured fibre texture, converted to A along the best axis: refuted if A > 0.64 (the cap is geometric: no bias acts). |

## 6. Accuracy against published measurements

Predictions were frozen (docs/PROTOCOL_LOG.md) before the sealed outcomes were opened. Held-out = opened only after the freeze; exposed = seen before the freeze (listed in the log); known = the known cases of PROBLEM.md; post hoc = scored with rules revised after opening (never counted as held-out).

**v1 — frozen before opening batches 1–2 (the held-out test)**

Conversion check (post hoc): the frozen v3 specs left the same-sample Js unlinked for 24 Br cases outside AlNiCo (they used the phase Js, which can only understate A). Rescored with the same-sample Js, 19 ceilings hold and 1 is exceeded: h2b_pmc7435735_baam_pps_br_not_reported (predicted none, A = 0.685) — an isotropic bonded magnet of rapidly solidified powder, whose remanence above 1/2 is exchange enhancement, excluded from A by definition; its phrase was not flagged by the v3 rules (fixed for later versions).
